LIST OF HINDI VIDEO SONGS ON MY COMPUTER



**LIST OF HINDI VIDEO SONGS ON MY COMPUTER**



**Shrikant G. Talageri**



I have a massive collection of songs in my computer: mainly Hindi and Marathi, video and audio, systematically arranged in folders and files, but also not-so-well-arranged Classical (Hindustani and Carnatic) and assorted semiclassical songs and folk songs (in other languages as well). This is apart from three cupboards full of my favorite books, mainly novels and storybooks. They are the solace of my life.

But I have recently become bitten by the uploading itch: I feel that one never knows when the end will come, and all this wealth of music and books will become redundant. So (senseless though it may be) I feel I should at least upload *lists* of the songs in my computer. If nothing else, people interested in old Hindi and Marathi songs (there are *very* few new ones in my lists) can at least go through the lists and see the names and titles of singers and songs that they used to hear in the past but whose existence they may have almost forgotten, and perhaps this may kindle nostalgic and happy memories. \[Actually I do have bigger and more obscure collections, mostly audios, but they also are not-so-well-arranged\].



At any rate, any itch begs to be scratched: so here is my second list: a **list of the Hindi Video Songs on my computer**:

\[The number 3/ 5/ 7 after any song represents the number of beats in the **tāl** of that song.

In multisinger songs, a number 3 or more before the song indicates the number of singers.

Music Directors’ names are given cryptically: for example, CR is C Ramchandra, SJ are Shankar Jaikishan, etc. but song lovers will recognize who is meant.

As in my article just before this:

Spelling mistakes, etc. should be excused: I do not have the patience to sit down and correct all the lists.

Likewise, if any song is repeated in the lists, I am sorry for that

p.s. a **few** songs added after this list was prepared may be missing in the lists\]
